Generate ONE fresh English experience rule revision from this batch's TRAINING material only. No old experience, reference patch, test trajectory, tools, alternatives, retries or extra judge.
Output only rules and unresolved under the typed schema. Python copies version/requires/excludes/applicability from the separately source-reviewed training analysis. Do not output or alter those fields. If that scope is unsupported, report an unresolved policy/applicability issue rather than assuming it met.
Use 5-7 concise rules, each body once and at most 480 characters. Cover applicability, event_relation, verification, preservation and exclusion. Python numbers and concatenates exact bodies; it does not repair policy. No duplicated full text, model-written rule IDs or directly copyable patches.
support/conditions/counterevidence contain existing evidence IDs only; preserve contains constraint IDs only. Put explanation prose in explanation fields. Normative rules require genuinely relevant documented requirements, source facts or preservation inferences, not observations alone. Cover every preservation constraint and mandatory counterevidence. ID existence does not prove entailment.
Delivered bodies must not contain case IDs, grading identifiers, file paths, evidence-management IDs, specialized field/function names or code patches. Express ordinary temporal guidance, not a unique implementation when alternatives preserve the same relations.
Read the full task/docs/original source, both actual AUTO patches and final source, complete expected/actual checks and event traces. Separate original successes, actual repair successes, improvements, regressions, source facts, policy inferences, causal hypotheses and unknowns. Defective starting behavior is not automatically preservation policy. Repair-Agent claims cannot override source or execution. No controlled intervention isolated causes; do not claim verified causes from assertion names or co-occurring changes.
The named evaluator adapter labels preservation inferences instead of pretending all policies are literal documentation. Check actual task/source anchors. Do not create business policy to explain expected results. Narrow unsupported scope or retain an unresolved issue. Unknown cause is not unknown policy; a missing proven stable repair is not itself a policy contradiction.
Use predicates literally. Distinguish accepted durable work, action-entry values and current-view capabilities. Generic resource ownership does not establish ownership of other state. Preserve unrelated consumers' documented boundaries. The question is whether a grounded candidate improves fixed baselines while recording every loss.
Development is not independent testing or stability proof. Review is execution-agent AI-assisted, not independent human approval. Write all generated prose in English.
The complete training payload uses lossless repeated-subtree references: recursively replace each object containing only $same with its identical shared_values item. These keys are not evidence IDs. No source, check, trace, success, regression or unknown is removed. Non-ASCII evidence/test values are losslessly JSON-escaped, not translated or edited:
{"serialization":"lossless-repeated-subtree-references-1","shared_values":{"D0001":"The museum loan approval editor currently writes on every keystroke. Coalesce edits using the trailing-task utility: after a 120 ms quiet interval write only the latest text in that editing burst. Subsequent bursts must continue to save. Preserve other supported behavior and the synchronous public interfaces. The adjacent search preview is outside this maintenance change.\n","D0002":{"role":"training_task","content":{"$same":"D0001"},"artifact":"inputs/task.md","sha256":"5dd12389dbd7e147b0563eb5828c5913c8f4ebcd3d7ec491399f752304d27bfc"},"D0003":"# Museum loan approval\n\n\nThe workspace edits persistent documents and submits snapshots to a review queue.\nThe public API is synchronous: open(id), edit(text), close(), submit(), dispose().\nReturning to a document creates a fresh editor visit and restores persisted text.\nThe adjacent search preview publishes after a 40 ms quiet interval; hiding it\ncancels unpublished work and later queries remain usable.\n\nThe coordinator owns editor visits; the acquired confirmation capability owns a view outlet. The document repository and durable queue are separate services. Component or native source defines the local service contracts.\nTheir local interface contracts define the behavior to preserve.\n\nThe injected store has read/write/subscribe, the queue has enqueue/list/subscribe,\nand timers have schedule(callback, delay)/cancel(handle). Render functions are\noutput sinks and do not call application code. Keep the workspace API and these\ninjected interfaces. A submission returns the queue receipt, or null when no\neditor is selected. Dispose detaches integrations before closing the workspace.\n\nDataclass editor visit and frozen receipt value; confirmation capability owns a closure over the current native visit.\n\nAn outlet acquires the visit active when binding is called; it cannot infer an earlier action from a receipt. Each opening creates a new identity. Native RPC only adapts timer and passive output/integration resource calls; storage, queue delivery, acquired outlet and command state execute in the target runtime.\n","D0004":{"role":"training_document","content":{"$same":"D0003"},"artifact":"inputs/source/README.md","sha256":"a197715d3a9b1eaffa1140d3ac8a221ff45c463e091085271ddb76b9e55764b4"},"D0005":"\"\"\"Accession editor and its injected workspace services.\"\"\"\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n\nclass Store:\n    def __init__(self, records):\n        self.records = dict(records)\n        self.listeners = {}\n        self.serial = self.revision = 0\n\n    def read(self, key):\n        return self.records.get(key, '')\n\n    def subscribe(self, listener):\n        self.serial += 1\n        token = self.serial\n        self.listeners[token] = listener\n        return token\n\n    def stop(self, token):\n        self.listeners.pop(token, None)\n\n    def write(self, key, text):\n        self.records[key] = text\n        self.revision += 1\n        event = dict(id=key, text=text, revision=self.revision)\n        for token, listener in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:\n                listener(event)\n        return event\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Receipt:\n    id: str\n    text: str\n\n\nclass Archive:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.receipts = [];self.listeners={};self.serial=0\n\n    def capture(self, value):\n        receipt = Receipt(value['id'], value['text'])\n        self.receipts.append(receipt)\n        event=vars(receipt).copy()\n        for token,fn in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:fn(event)\n        return event\n\n    def subscribe(self,fn):\n        self.serial+=1;self.listeners[self.serial]=fn;return self.serial\n    def stop(self,token):self.listeners.pop(token,None)\n    def list(self):\n        return [vars(receipt).copy() for receipt in self.receipts]\n\n\nclass Trailing:\n    def __init__(self, env, delay, work):\n        self.env, self.delay, self.work = env, delay, work\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n    def push(self, value):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.pending = value\n        self.handle = self.env.schedule(self.flush, self.delay)\n\n    def flush(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        value = self.pending\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n        if value is not None:\n            self.work(value)\n\n    def cancel(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n\nclass Confirmation:\n    def __init__(self,current,emit):self.current=current;self.emit=emit;self.owner=current()\n    def active(self):return self.owner is not None and self.owner is self.current()\n    def show(self,receipt):\n        if self.active():self.emit(receipt)\n\n@dataclass\nclass Visit:\n    id: str\n    text: str\n    token: int\n    task: Trailing\n\n\nclass Workspace:\n    def __init__(self, env):\n        self.env, self.current, self.navigation = env, None, 0\n\n    def retire(self):\n        leaving, self.current = self.current, None\n        if leaving:\n            self.env.store.stop(leaving.token)\n        self.env.output('document', None)\n        pass\n\n    def open(self, key):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n        self.navigation += 1\n        request = self.navigation\n        self.retire()\n        pass\n        token = self.env.store.subscribe(lambda event: self.env.output('status', event) if event['id'] == key else None)\n        task = Trailing(self.env, 120, lambda text: self.env.write(key, text))\n        self.current = Visit(key, self.env.store.read(key), token, task)\n        self.env.output('document', dict(id=key, text=self.current.text))\n\n    def edit(self, text):\n        if self.current is not None:\n            self.current.text = text\n            self.env.write(self.current.id, text)\n\n    def close(self):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n        self.navigation += 1\n        self.retire()\n\n    def submit(self):\n        confirmation=Confirmation(lambda:self.current,lambda receipt:self.env.output('notice',receipt))\n        visit = self.current\n        if visit is None:\n            return None\n        snapshot = dict(id=visit.id, text=visit.text)\n        pass\n        pass\n        pass\n        pass\n        receipt = self.env.capture(snapshot)\n        pass\n        confirmation.show(receipt)\n        return receipt\n\n    def dispose(self):\n        self.close()\n\n\nclass Preview:\n    def __init__(self, env):\n        self.task = Trailing(env, 40, lambda query: env.output('preview', dict(query=query)))\n\n    def query(self, text):\n        self.task.push(text)\n\n    def hide(self):\n        self.task.cancel()\n","D0006":{"role":"training_source","content":{"$same":"D0005"},"artifact":"inputs/source/src/workflow.py","sha256":"fcbfd9630a0105fc56fa5a351487909e98f0fd8567c6f187eae854bfcfc1267e"},"D0007":{"effect":"reentrant-switch-on-flush","expected":true,"description":"A `src/workflow.py` so editor edits use the existing per-visit 120 ms trailing task instead of writing per keystroke. Pending editor text is flushed before a visit retires (close, replacement open, dispose) and before submission, preserving persisted reopening and submission snapshots. Search preview behavior was unchanged. editor and its injected workspace services.\"\"\"\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n\nclass Store:\n    def __init__(self, records):\n        self.records = dict(records)\n        self.listeners = {}\n        self.serial = self.revision = 0\n\n    def read(self, key):\n        return self.records.get(key, '')\n\n    def subscribe(self, listener):\n        self.serial += 1\n        token = self.serial\n        self.listeners[token] = listener\n        return token\n\n    def stop(self, token):\n        self.listeners.pop(token, None)\n\n    def write(self, key, text):\n        self.records[key] = text\n        self.revision += 1\n        event = dict(id=key, text=text, revision=self.revision)\n        for token, listener in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:\n                listener(event)\n        return event\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Receipt:\n    id: str\n    text: str\n\n\nclass Archive:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.receipts = [];self.listeners={};self.serial=0\n\n    def capture(self, value):\n        receipt = Receipt(value['id'], value['text'])\n        self.receipts.append(receipt)\n        event=vars(receipt).copy()\n        for token,fn in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:fn(event)\n        return event\n\n    def subscribe(self,fn):\n        self.serial+=1;self.listeners[self.serial]=fn;return self.serial\n    def stop(self,token):self.listeners.pop(token,None)\n    def list(self):\n        return [vars(receipt).copy() for receipt in self.receipts]\n\n\nclass Trailing:\n    def __init__(self, env, delay, work):\n        self.env, self.delay, self.work = env, delay, work\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n    def push(self, value):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.pending = value\n        self.handle = self.env.schedule(self.flush, self.delay)\n\n    def flush(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        value = self.pending\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n        if value is not self.current.task.push(text)\n\n    def close(self):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n        self.navigation += 1\n        self.retire()\n\n    def submit(self):\n editor and its injected workspace services.\"\"\"\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n\nclass Store:\n    def __init__(self, records):\n        self.records = dict(records)\n        self.listeners = {}\n        self.serial = self.revision = 0\n\n    def read(self, key):\n        return self.records.get(key, '')\n\n    def subscribe(self, listener):\n        self.serial += 1\n        token = self.serial\n        self.listeners[token] = listener\n        return token\n\n    def stop(self, token):\n        self.listeners.pop(token, None)\n\n    def write(self, key, text):\n        self.records[key] = text\n        self.revision += 1\n        event = dict(id=key, text=text, revision=self.revision)\n        for token, listener in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:\n                listener(event)\n        return event\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Receipt:\n    id: str\n    text: str\n\n\nclass Archive:\n    def __init__(self):\n event=vars(receipt).copy()\n        for token,fn in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:fn(event)\n        return event\n\n    def subscribe(self,fn):\n        self.serial+=1;self.listeners[self.serial]=fn;return self.serial\n    def stop(self,token):self.listeners.pop(token,None)\n    def list(self):\n        return [vars(receipt).copy() for receipt in self.receipts]\n\n\nclass Trailing:\n    def __init__(self, env, delay, work):\n        self.env, self.delay, self.work = env, delay, work\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n    def push(self, value):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.pending = value\n        self.handle = self.env.schedule(self.flush, self.delay)\n\n    def flush(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        value = self.pending\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n        if value is not None:\n            self.work(value)\n\n    def cancel(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n\nclass Confirmation:\n    def __init__(self,current,emit):self.current=current;self.emit=emit;self.owner=current()\n    def active(self):return self.owner is not None and self.owner is self.current()\n    def show(self,receipt):\n        if self.active():self.emit(receipt)\n\n@dataclass\nclass Visit:\n    id: str\n    text: str\n    token: int\n    task: Trailing\n\n\nclass Workspace:\n    def __init__(self, env):\n        self.env, self.current, self.navigation = env, None, 0\n\n    def retire(self):\n        leaving, self.current = self.current, None\n        if leaving:\n            leaving.task.flush()\n            self.env.store.stop(leaving.token)\n        self.env.output('document', None)\n\n    def open(self, key):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n        self.navigation += 1\n        request = self.navigation\n        self.retire()\n        token = self.env.store.subscribe(lambda event: self.env.output('status', event) if event['id'] == key else None)\n        task = Trailing(self.env, 120, lambda text: self.env.write(key, text))\n        self.current = Visit(key, self.env.store.read(key), token, task)\n        self.env.output('document', dict(id=key, text=self.current.text))\n\n    def edit(self, text):\n        if self.current is not None:\n            self.current.text = text\n            self.current.task.push(text)\n\n    def close(self):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n        self.navigation += 1\n        self.retire()\n\n    def submit(self):\n        confirmation=Confirmation(lambda:self.current,lambda receipt:self.env.output('notice',receipt))\n        visit = self.current\n        if visit is None:\n            return None\n        visit.task.flush()\n        snapshot = dict(id=visit.id, text=visit.text)\n        receipt = self.env.capture(snapshot)\n        confirmation.show(receipt)\n        return receipt\n\n    def dispose(self):\n        self.close()\n\n\nclass Preview:\n    def cause independently verified; see evidence ledger for separately authored source reasoning."},"D0416":"The museum loan approval editor currently writes on every keystroke. Coalesce edits using the trailing-task utility: after a 120 ms quiet interval write only the latest text in that editing burst. Subsequent bursts must continue to save. Preserve other supported behavior and the synchronous public interfaces. The adjacent search preview is outside this maintenance change.","D0417":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/task.md","start":1,"end":1,"quote":{"$same":"D0416"}},"D0418":[{"$same":"D0417"}],"D0419":{"id":"R-task","kind":"documented_requirement","statement":"Coalesce editor input into latest-only 120ms quiet saves; later bursts remain usable. Preserve supported behavior and synchronous public interfaces; preview is outside scope.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0418"}},"D0420":"# Museum loan approval\n\n\nThe workspace edits persistent documents and submits snapshots to a review queue.\nThe public API is synchronous: open(id), edit(text), close(), submit(), dispose().\nReturning to a document creates a fresh editor visit and restores persisted text.\nThe adjacent search preview publishes after a 40 ms quiet interval; hiding it\ncancels unpublished work and later queries remain usable.\n\nThe coordinator owns editor visits; the acquired confirmation capability owns a view outlet. The document repository and durable queue are separate services. Component or native source defines the local service contracts.\nTheir local interface contracts define the behavior to preserve.\n\nThe injected store has read/write/subscribe, the queue has enqueue/list/subscribe,\nand timers have schedule(callback, delay)/cancel(handle). Render functions are\noutput sinks and do not call application code. Keep the workspace API and these\ninjected interfaces. A submission returns the queue receipt, or null when no\neditor is selected. Dispose detaches integrations before closing the workspace.\n\nDataclass editor visit and frozen receipt value; confirmation capability owns a closure over the current native visit.\n\nAn outlet acquires the visit active when binding is called; it cannot infer an earlier action from a receipt. Each opening creates a new identity. Native RPC only adapts timer and passive output/integration resource calls; storage, queue delivery, acquired outlet and command state execute in the target runtime.","D0421":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/README.md","start":1,"end":21,"quote":{"$same":"D0420"}},"D0422":[{"$same":"D0421"}],"D0423":{"id":"R-doc","kind":"documented_requirement","statement":"Documents and durable queue are separate services. Preserve synchronous APIs and injected contracts; reopening creates a fresh visit and restores persisted text. Acquired outlets own visits.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0422"}},"D0424":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/README.md","start":21,"end":21,"quote":"An outlet acquires the visit active when binding is called; it cannot infer an earlier action from a receipt. Each opening creates a new identity. Native RPC only adapts timer and passive output/integration resource calls; storage, queue delivery, acquired outlet and command state execute in the target runtime."},"D0425":[{"$same":"D0424"}],"D0426":{"id":"R-outlet","kind":"documented_requirement","statement":"An outlet captures the visit active when binding is called, cannot infer an earlier action from a receipt, and same-document reopening creates a new identity.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0425"}},"D0427":"class Store:\n    def __init__(self, records):\n        self.records = dict(records)\n        self.listeners = {}\n        self.serial = self.revision = 0\n\n    def read(self, key):\n        return self.records.get(key, '')\n\n    def subscribe(self, listener):\n        self.serial += 1\n        token = self.serial\n        self.listeners[token] = listener\n        return token\n\n    def stop(self, token):\n        self.listeners.pop(token, None)\n\n    def write(self, key, text):\n        self.records[key] = text\n        self.revision += 1\n        event = dict(id=key, text=text, revision=self.revision)\n        for token, listener in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:\n                listener(event)\n        return event","D0428":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/src/workflow.py","start":5,"end":30,"quote":{"$same":"D0427"}},"D0429":[{"$same":"D0428"}],"D0430":{"id":"F-store","kind":"source_fact","statement":"Storage updates document and revision before synchronously invoking still-subscribed listeners; it does not define UI ownership.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0429"}},"D0431":"class Archive:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.receipts = [];self.listeners={};self.serial=0\n\n    def capture(self, value):\n        receipt = Receipt(value['id'], value['text'])\n        self.receipts.append(receipt)\n        event=vars(receipt).copy()\n        for token,fn in list(self.listeners.items()):\n            if token in self.listeners:fn(event)\n        return event\n\n    def subscribe(self,fn):\n        self.serial+=1;self.listeners[self.serial]=fn;return self.serial\n    def stop(self,token):self.listeners.pop(token,None)\n    def list(self):\n        return [vars(receipt).copy() for receipt in self.receipts]","D0432":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/src/workflow.py","start":39,"end":55,"quote":{"$same":"D0431"}},"D0433":[{"$same":"D0432"}],"D0434":{"id":"F-queue","kind":"source_fact","statement":"Queue capture constructs and retains an immutable receipt, then synchronously notifies integrations. Repeated calls append distinct accepted actions.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0433"}},"D0435":"class Trailing:\n    def __init__(self, env, delay, work):\n        self.env, self.delay, self.work = env, delay, work\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n\n    def push(self, value):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.pending = value\n        self.handle = self.env.schedule(self.flush, self.delay)\n\n    def flush(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        value = self.pending\n        self.handle = self.pending = None\n        if value is not None:\n            self.work(value)\n\n    def cancel(self):\n        if self.handle is not None:\n            self.env.cancel(self.handle)\n        self.handle = self.pending = None","D0436":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/src/workflow.py","start":58,"end":80,"quote":{"$same":"D0435"}},"D0437":[{"$same":"D0436"}],"D0438":{"id":"F-batch","kind":"source_fact","statement":"Pending value and timer are cleared before work; cancel discards pending work. Empty text differs from no pending value.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0437"}},"D0439":{"type":"lines","file":"inputs/source/src/workflow.py","start":83,"end":87,"quote":"class Confirmation:\n    def __init__(self,current,emit):self.current=current;self.emit=emit;self.owner=current()\n    def active(self):return self.owner is not None and self.owner is self.current()\n    def show(self,receipt):\n        if self.active():self.emit(receipt)"},"D0440":[{"$same":"D0439"}],"D0441":{"id":"F-capability","kind":"source_fact","statement":"Acquisition captures the current native visit. Later display compares its exact owner identity with the current visit.","author":"execution_agent_named_review_evaluation_adapter_AI_assisted","related":[],"anchors":{"$same":"D0440"}},"D0442":"class Workspace:\n    def __init__(self, env):\n        self.env, self.current, self.navigation = env, None, 0\n\n    def retire(self):\n        leaving, self.current = self.current, None\n        if leaving:\n            self.env.store.stop(leaving.token)\n        self.env.output('document', None)\n        pass\n\n    def open(self, key):\n        self.env.output('notice', None)\n
